# 2016 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** a story that dies in 24h
**Star:** IG Stories · `itt16-ig-stories` · `sites/instagram/stories.html`
**ILS June:** 1,045,534,808 · users BLANK
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 223
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt16-ig-stories`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2016/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2016/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2016 — dual scale · bans
2. Instagram Stories — 24h slide
3. Pokémon GO — leftover sidewalks
4. Reactions — five faces
5. WhatsApp E2E — default lock
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt16-ig-stories`

**Room:** `years/2016/sites/instagram/stories.html`
**Hook:** `data-ig-story`
**Nostalgia:** IG Stories — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty slide · TikTok-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt16-ig-stories`.

**Complete writes**
- Add a 24h slide. Writes itt16-ig-stories.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2016` · key `itt16-ig-stories`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-ig-stories` — do not write `itt15-*` or `itt17-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Pokémon GO → `years/2016/sites/pokemongo/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 223 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt16-alphago-about` · sites/alphago/about.html

**Open:** `/years/2016/sites/alphago/about.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="alphago-about"])`
**Need:** needPick=`alphago` · field ≥2 (placeholder “alphago leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `youtube` · `facebook` · `instagram`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="alphago-about"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt16-alphago-about']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `alphago`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt16-ig-stories` stays empty. `itt15-alphago-about` and `itt17-alphago-about` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="alphago"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “alphago leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="alphago-about"]`.
- `localStorage['itt16-alphago-about']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2016", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt16-alphago-about`.
- Does **not** write `itt16-ig-stories`.
- **Next:** ★ gold → `/years/2016/sites/instagram/stories.html`
